We further find that English-only OPD can also increase performance for Korean and Japanese, but often shifts the responses toward English, highlighting the importance of multilingual data to preserving target-language responses.

</details>


### [148] [Hierarchical Latent Prediction for Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05806)
*Chang Shi,Tim Pearce,Manan Tomar,Siddhartha Sen,John Langford*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出层级潜在预测（HiLP），通过引入更高层级的抽象潜在表示来辅助学习，从而减少潜在空间滚动中的误差累积效应，改善长程推理与规划能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准的Next-Token Prediction（NTP）采用教师强制训练，在长程推理和规划任务上可能不是最优的。虽然已有MTP和NextLat等工作试图通过预测多个未来token或潜在空间自监督预测来缓解问题，但这些辅助目标要么视野有限（horizon有限），要么因多步滚动而产生复合误差累积。因此需要一个能减少误差累积且具备更长预测视野的方法。

Method: 提出HiLP（Hierarchical Latent Prediction），在原有潜在预测框架中引入一个更高层级的抽象潜在表示作为辅助监督信号。该高层级潜在表示有助于在潜在空间滚动过程中降低误差累积效应，使得模型能够进行更长视界的连贯信念状态表征与预测。

Result: 实验表明HiLP能够实现更长视界的连贯信念状态表征，在编码和多种多步推理基准上均表现优于基线，并且提升了推测解码（speculative decoding）的效率。

Conclusion: HiLP通过层级化的潜在预测机制有效缓解了潜在空间滚动中的误差累积问题，在长程推理和编码任务上取得了显著效果，同时还能带来推测解码的效率优势。

Abstract: While standard Next-Token Prediction (NTP) lays the foundation of language model pre- training, its teacher-forced training paradigm may not be optimal for long-horizon reasoning and planning. Recent works such as Multi-Token Prediction (MTP) and Next-Latent prediction (NextLat) try to mitigate the problem through predicting multiple future tokens and self-supervised prediction in the latent space. However, those auxiliary objectives either have a limited horizon or suffer from compounding error from multi-step rollout. We introduce Hierarchical Latent Prediction (HiLP), which introduces an auxiliary higher-level abstract latent to help reduce the error accumulation effect in latent-space rollouts. Experiments show that HiLP can lead to longer-horizon coherent belief state representation and demonstrate the effectiveness of our method across coding and multi-step reasoning benchmarks, and offers more speculative decoding efficiency.

</details>


### [149] [M$^3$R-Bench: A Unified Benchmark for Evidence-Grounded Multimodal Metaphor Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05817)
*Hong Jiang,Junnan Zhu,Jingwang Huang,Xiao Sun,Yuming Yang,Jiang Zhong,Ruirui Chen,Jingman Shi,Hao Wu,Nayu Liu,Xinyi Jiang,Kaiwen Wei*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了M³R-Bench，一个包含1000个图文实例的统一且基于证据的多模态隐喻理解基准，并提供M³R-Reasoner方法（结合课程式推理监督与任务感知强化学习），以8B参数规模在多项指标上超越更大的专有MLLM模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有隐喻理解基准主要依赖孤立子任务进行评估，缺乏基于证据的解释，难以判断模型是否真正建立了基于视觉和文本线索的跨模态映射。

Method: 构建M³R-Bench基准，包含隐喻出现、目标-源映射、情感和分阶段解释（证据识别-映射建立-情感推理）的联合标注；提出M³R-Reasoner，结合课程式推理监督与任务感知强化学习。

Result: M³R-Reasoner以8B参数超越更大的专有MLLM，在视觉证据和情感推理得分上分别比GPT-5.5高28.45和30.11分，在平均评分上比Claude-Sonnet-4.6高8.00分。

Conclusion: 现有模型存在跨模态证据-映射不匹配问题，M³R-Reasoner通过对齐模型推理与隐喻解释过程有效缓解了这一缺陷，展示了小模型在统一多模态隐喻推理任务上的潜力。

Abstract: Metaphor enables the understanding of abstract concepts through cross-domain mappings while conveying affective attitudes. In multimodal scenarios, visual and textual information jointly construct Target--Source mappings, requiring both conceptual understanding and cross-modal reasoning. However, existing benchmarks mainly evaluate metaphor understanding through isolated subtasks and lack evidence-grounded explanations, making it difficult to assess whether models establish mappings grounded in visual and textual cues.To address these limitations, we introduce M$^3$R-Bench, a unified and evidence-grounded benchmark containing 1,000 image--text instances with human-verified annotations. Guided by Conceptual Metaphor Theory and theories of nonliteral language understanding, M$^3$R-Bench provides joint annotations for metaphor occurrence, Target--Source mapping, sentiment, and stage-wise explanations following ``evidence identification--mapping establishment--sentiment inference.''Evaluations on M$^3$R-Bench reveal that existing models often overlook visual evidence, rely on superficial textual cues, and produce inaccurate Target--Source mappings, exposing a cross-modal evidence--mapping mismatch. To address this mismatch, we propose M$^3$R-Reasoner, which combines curriculum-based reasoning supervision with task-aware reinforcement learning to align model reasoning with metaphor interpretation. Experiments show that, with only an 8B-parameter backbone, M$^3$R-Reasoner outperforms larger proprietary MLLMs across four unified-task metrics and improves Visual Evidence and Sentiment Justification scores over GPT-5.5 by 28.45 and 30.11 points, respectively, while surpassing Claude-Sonnet-4.6 by 8.00 points in mean rubric score. The dataset and code are available at https://github.com/hongshi4/M3R-Bench.

</details>


### [150] [Decomposed Entailment for Factuality Checking and Hallucination Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05823)
*Achir Oukelmoun,Nasredine Semmar,Gaël De Chalendar*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: HallDetect是一个轻量级、无需参考、黑盒的幻觉检测框架，通过将生成内容分解为原子声明，并用紧凑的编码器蕴含模型结合多尺度源块对比验证及非对称聚合评分来检测幻觉，在三个基准上优于同资源基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型常因事实不一致（如幻觉）而可靠性受损，现有检测方法在资源消耗、参考依赖及适用场景上存在局限，需要更高效通用的幻觉检测方案。

Method: HallDetect采用基于分解的事实性评估：将生成内容拆分为原子声明，每个声明通过紧凑的编码器蕴含模型验证，使用对比公式结合多尺度源块库，并通过非对称评分聚合——单个被自信反驳的声明即触发响应标记。所有方法共享4-bit量化骨干和消费级硬件预算。

Result: 在受控协议下（所有方法共享相同量化骨干与硬件预算），HallDetect在四个基准中的三个上优于同资源的生成式和嵌入基线，在不同骨干家族间保持稳定，并能提供声明到句段的审计追踪以定位每个错误。

Conclusion: HallDetect证明了在受限资源条件下，轻量级编码器方法在幻觉检测上可超越生成式基线，同时保持跨骨干的稳定性并提供可解释的错误定位能力，适用于更广泛的源锚定生成场景。

Abstract: The reliability of Large Language Models (LLMs) is often compromised by factual inconsistencies, including hallucinations---cases where generated content is not supported by the underlying source. We present HallDetect, a lightweight, reference-free, and black-box framework for hallucination detection that we evaluate not only on summarization but across a broader range of source-grounded generation settings. HallDetect builds on decomposition-based factuality evaluation: generated content is decomposed into atomic claims, each verified by a compact encoder-based entailment model through a contrastive formulation over a multi-scale library of source chunks, and aggregated with an asymmetric score in which a single confidently contradicted claim flags the response. Under a controlled protocol in which all methods share the same 4-bit quantized backbones and consumer-grade hardware budget, HallDetect outperforms comparably resourced generative and embedding-based baselines on three of four benchmarks while remaining stable across backbone families, and yields a claim-to-span audit trail that localizes each error.

</details>


### [151] [MoCA: Implicit Social Context Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05825)
*Wenhao Xu,Kaiwen Zhang,Hao Li,Maowei You,Yongzheng Ji,Siyuan Zuo,Jingxuan Yu,Sina A,Xinyao Tan,Bobo Li,Hao Fei,Mong-Li Lee,Wynne Hsu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了隐式社交语境分析（MoCA）任务，系统建模隐式社交场景中的情感、意图和立场三个维度，构建了包含3108个多模态实例的高质量基准数据集。针对多模态大语言模型在此任务上的不足，提出了冲突驱动溯因推理（CoDAR）框架，通过模拟观察表达与预期真实行为之间的认知冲突来推断隐藏心理状态，实验表明该方法显著提升了模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类社交沟通（如情感和意图）往往以高度隐式的方式传达，其深层含义通过间接的、基于社会和文化背景的信号而非明确陈述来表达。这类隐式社交语境在现实交互中普遍存在，但缺乏正式的、系统化的研究框架。

Method: 1）提出MoCA任务，从情感（affection）、意图（intent）和立场（stance）三个关键维度系统建模隐式社交场景；2）构建包含3108个真实世界采集的多模态实例的高质量基准，并带有细粒度认知标注（谁对谁表达了什么，以及如何表达和为何表达）；3）提出CoDAR（Conflict-Driven Abductive Reasoning）框架，将被观察的表达与预期真实行为之间的差异建模为认知冲突，从而推断隐藏的心理状态。

Result: 最先进的多模态大语言模型在该任务上表现显著不足，主要依赖于显式线索，对潜在社交语境的推理能力有限。CoDAR框架大幅提升了模型性能，但与人类推理能力之间仍存在较大差距。

Conclusion: 隐式社交理解是当前多模态模型面临的根本性难题。尽管CoDAR框架带来了显著改进，但模型与人类推理之间仍存在巨大鸿沟，凸显了隐式社交理解这一任务的深层难度，也为未来研究指明了方向。

Abstract: Human social communication, such as affection and intent, is often conveyed in highly implicit ways, where underlying meanings are expressed through indirect, socially and culturally grounded signals rather than explicit statements. Such implicit social contexts are pervasive in real-world interactions, yet there remains a lack of a formal and systematic framework for studying them. In this paper, we introduce Implicit Social Context Analysis (MoCA), a novel task that systematically models implicit social scenarios along three key dimensions: affection, intent, and stance. We construct a high-quality benchmark containing 3,108 multimodal instances collected from real-world sources, with fine-grained cognitive annotations revealing who expresses what toward whom, as well as how and why it is conveyed. Using the MoCA dataset, we show that state-of-the-art multimodal large language models struggle significantly with this task because of their reliance on explicit cues and limited ability to reason over latent social contexts. To address this challenge, we propose Conflict-Driven Abductive Reasoning (CoDAR), a novel framework that models the discrepancy between observed expressions and expected truthful behavior as cognitive conflict, thereby enabling the inference of hidden mental states. Extensive experiments demonstrate that CoDAR substantially improves model performance. Nevertheless, a large gap from human reasoning remains, highlighting the fundamental difficulty of implicit social understanding.

</details>


### [152] [Enhancing Social Intelligence in LLMs with Hierarchical Reasoning and Utterance-Level Goal Rewarding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05832)
*Xiaofeng Wang,Kakam Chong,Shuai Xiao,DeXin Kong,Qingyuan Tian,Chen Ju,Xu Yan,Shuai Zhao,Fei Huang,Rui Wang,Shuguang Han,jufeng chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出TSR框架（Think-Strategy-Response），将社交对话分解为高层策略规划和低层语言执行两个层次，并引入LHRL-VGR算法（线性化分层强化学习与方差门控奖励），通过动态路由奖励来平衡目标完成和策略遵从。在SOTOPIA基准上，7B参数的Qwen2.5模型超越了GPT-4o基线7.32%的目标完成成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在结构化任务中表现出色，但在动态社交互动中表现不佳，因为社交互动需要长期目标协调和快速适应。现有方法对所有话语施加统一的目标导向奖励，忽略了每个对话回合目标的特异性，也未考虑潜在策略的合理性依据。此外，行为计划理论（TPB）为理解意图与行为提供了理论支撑但尚未被应用于LLM社交对话优化。

Method: 1) 提出TSR框架，受计划行为理论启发，将社交对话分解为两层：高层策略规划（Think）和低层语言执行（Strategy-Response)；2) 提出LHRL-VGR算法，一种线性化分层强化学习算法，根据目标达成分数的方差动态路由奖励，平衡目标完成和策略遵从两个维度的优化信号。

Result: 在SOTOPIA基准上，基于Qwen2.5-7B微调的智能体在目标完成成功率上超越GPT-4o基线7.32%，在多智能体社交协商任务中达到最先进水平。

Conclusion: 将社交对话内在地分层为策略规划与语言执行，并采用方差门控的奖励路由机制，能够有效提升LLM在多智能体社交协商中的目标达成能力，且小模型可通过该方法超越大模型基线。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) excel in structured tasks but struggle with dynamic social interactions, where success requires long-term goal coordination and rapid adaptation. Current methods often apply uniform goal-based rewards to every utterance, overlooking the specificity of objectives at each dialogue turn and failing to account for the rationale of potential strategies. Inspired by the Theory of Planned Behavior, we propose the Think-Strategy-Response (TSR) framework, which decomposes social dialogue into two hierarchical stages: high-level strategic planning and low-level linguistic execution. To optimize TSR, we introduce Linearized Hierarchical Reinforcement Learning with Variance-Gated Rewards (LHRL-VGR), a novel algorithm that dynamically routes rewards - balancing goal completion and strategy adherence - based on the variance of goal achievement scores. Experiments on the SOTOPIA benchmark show that our approach fine-tunes a Qwen2.5-7B agent to surpass the GPT-4o baseline by 7.32% in goal completion success, demonstrating state-of-the-art performance in multi-agent social negotiation tasks.

</details>


### [153] [MameLoshnLM: Yiddish Language Model and Evaluation Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05850)
*Uri Katz,Omer Goldman,Tomasz Limisiewicz,Reut Tsarfaty,Noah A. Smith*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了MameLoshnLM，这是首个专门为意第绪语构建的开源80亿参数语言模型。作者创建了Oytser高质量预训练语料库和Kashes多任务基准，通过在Llama 3.1 8B上继续预训练获得该模型，在多项任务上优于同规模开放基线，并展现出更好的词汇和形态学特征捕捉能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 意第绪语虽有丰富的文学传统，但数字资源稀缺、可靠的评测基准匮乏，现有多语言语料库和基准包含大量噪声、机器翻译和错误分类的文本，不适用于意第绪语建模。

Method: 1) 构建Oytser高质量意第绪语预训练语料库（结合当代网络文本和文学资料）；2) 创建Kashes多任务评测基准（涵盖翻译、语言学分析、信息抽取和语言理解）；3) 在Llama 3.1 8B基础上使用这些资源继续预训练得到MameLoshnLM。

Result: MameLoshnLM在Kashes基准的各类任务上优于同规模开放基线模型，且在词汇和形态学模式捕捉上比通用多语言模型表现更好，说明提升不仅是定量的更是定性的。

Conclusion: 这项工作既为意第绪语NLP提供了基础资源与模型，也为历史上丰富但数字化不足的语言开发大语言模型提供了实用模板，同时揭示了噪声网络级多语言数据在低资源语言上的普遍失败模式。

Abstract: We present MameLoshnLM, the first open-source 8B-parameter language model built specifically for Yiddish. Despite Yiddish's rich textual tradition, its limited digital presence and the scarcity of reliable evaluation resources have constrained progress in Yiddish language modeling. Existing multilingual corpora and benchmarks are often poor proxies for the language, containing substantial amounts of noisy, machine-translated, and misclassified text. We address these gaps by introducing Oytser, a high-quality Yiddish pretraining corpus that combines contemporary web-native sources with literary materials, and Kashes, a multi-task benchmark spanning translation, linguistic analysis, information extraction, and language understanding. Using these resources, we continue pretraining Llama 3.1 8B to obtain MameLoshnLM. Across the tasks in the benchmark, MameLoshnLM outperforms open baselines of similar scale. Our analyses show that these gains are not only quantitative: relative to general-purpose multilingual models, MameLoshnLM better captures language-defining lexical and morphological patterns, pointing to a broader failure mode of noisy web-scale multilingual data for low-resource languages. Our results provide both a foundation for Yiddish NLP and a practical template for language model development in historically rich but digitally underrepresented languages.

</details>


### [154] [Mapping Similarity Spaces across Embedding Models with Synthetic Query Probing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05857)
*Marcin Rozmus,Peter van der Putten*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Synthetic Query Probing方法，通过从文档生成查询来创建可控的查询-块对，实现在无参考标注条件下分析不同嵌入模型间相似度分数的映射关系。实验表明模型在排序上基本一致，但绝对分数存在系统性偏差，学习的映射函数（尤其等渗回归）能部分对齐分数空间并改善阈值可移植性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG系统依赖相似度分数检索内容，但不同嵌入模型的分数因几何特性差异而不可直接比较，这阻碍了模型迁移和阈值复用。现有方法缺乏大规模、无需人工标注的跨模型分数分析手段。

Method: 提出Synthetic Query Probing框架：从文档自动生成查询，构造受控的查询-块对；在多个嵌入配置上评估，使用线性、等渗和分位数映射学习分数转换函数；在SciFact和专有语料库上进行验证。

Result: 模型在排序上基本一致，但绝对分数存在系统性失真；学习到的映射能部分对齐分数空间并改善阈值可移植性，其中等渗回归（isotonic regression）表现最佳。

Conclusion: 研究凸显了跨模型校准的必要性，并将Synthetic Query Probing定位为可扩展的分析嵌入可比性框架，为解决RAG系统中分数不可比问题提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation systems rely on similarity scores to retrieve relevant content, yet scores are not directly comparable across embedding models due to differing geometric properties, complicating model migration and limiting threshold reuse. We study how similarity scores can be related by learning mappings between score distributions rather than embeddings. We introduce Synthetic Query Probing, generating queries from documents to create controlled query-chunk pairs, enabling large-scale, reference-free analysis of cross-model similarity behavior. We evaluate the approach on multiple embedding configurations and learn score conversion functions using linear, isotonic, and quantile mappings. Experiments on SciFact and a proprietary corpus show that while models largely agree on rankings, their absolute scores exhibit systematic distortions. Learned mappings partially align these spaces and improve threshold portability, with isotonic regression performing best. Our results highlight the need for cross-model calibration and position Synthetic Query Probing as a scalable framework for analyzing embedding comparability.

</details>


### [155] [MACRO: Markov Chain Routing of Transformer Layers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05872)
*Paweł Batorski,Abtin Pourhadi,Akylgali Aitaza,Przemysław Spurek,Paul Swoboda*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MACRO是一个无需修改模型权重即可为LLM学习任务特定层路由的框架，将层路由建模为上下文相关的马尔可夫策略，支持跳过、重复和残差隐藏状态加法操作，通过训练数据反馈更新路由分布并用top-k Viterbi算法解码。在多个推理和知识基准上平均提升5.0%准确率，比Dr. LLM提升7.2%且搜索时间减少9.4倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM逐层顺序执行，动态层路由（如层重复、跳过等）可提升性能，但已有方法通常需要更新模型权重、为每个测试实例运行昂贵的搜索循环，或推理时需要真实标签，这些都限制了动态层路由的实际应用。

Method: MACRO将层路由建模为上下文相关的马尔可夫策略，条件包括层索引、计算预算阶段、方向位移和算子上下文，支持跳过、重复和残差隐藏状态加法操作。路由分布通过训练数据反馈更新，并使用top-k Viterbi算法解码出高概率的候选程序。

Result: 在多个开源权重LLM的多样推理和知识基准上，MACRO相比无路由基线平均提升5.0%准确率，小模型获益最大。相比最佳动态路由方法Dr. LLM提升7.2%，同时将路由搜索时间从14.8小时降至1.6小时（减少9.4倍）。

Conclusion: MACRO证明无需修改模型参数即可有效学习任务特定的层路由路径，且相比现有动态路由方法在性能和效率上均有显著优势，为LLM推理的动态计算提供了高效可行的方案。

Abstract: Standard Large Language Models (LLMs) execute layers sequentially. Dynamic layer routing, i.e. search for a different execution path through layers involving layer repetitions, skips and other moves, can improve performance. Existing routing approaches often require updating model weights, running expensive search loops per test instance, or demand ground-truth labels during inference. In this work, we propose Markov Chain Routing of Transformer Layers (MACRO), a framework that learns task-specific routes over LLM architectures without modifying underlying parameters. MACRO models layer routing as a context-dependent Markov policy conditioned on layer indices, computation budget phases, directional displacements, and operator context, supporting skip, repeat, and residual hidden-state addition operations. The Markov route distribution is updated via feedback on training data and decoded using a top-k Viterbi algorithm to isolate high-probability candidate programs. We evaluate MACRO across diverse reasoning and knowledge benchmarks on multiple open-weight LLMs. MACRO achieves a +5.0% average accuracy improvement over the unrouted baselines, with largest gains on small models. We outperform the best dynamic routing approach Dr. LLM by +7.2%, while reducing route-search time 9.4x (from 14.8 to 1.6 hours). Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/Batorskq/MACRO.

</details>


### [156] [Causal Episodic Memory for Feedback-Driven Agent Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05906)
*Khang Nhat Hoang Vo,Tam Minh Chu,Anh Trac Duc Dinh,Thuyen Vinh Ha Bui,Tho Quan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出MERIT，一种无训练的Text-to-SQL修复智能体，通过维护在线双极性记忆（oracle验证的成功修正和观察到的失败方向）来提升后续修复效果。在Spider上执行准确率从66.34%提升至69.79%，在BIRD上从47.35%提升至48.44%，但后者提升证据较弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体在修复失败时经常丢弃成功的修正，导致后续任务重新发现相似解决方案，造成计算浪费。作者希望研究在无需更新模型参数的情况下，利用最终修复结果改进后续Text-to-SQL任务。

Method: MERIT采用训练无关的方法，维护一个oracle验证的成功修正和观察到的失败方向的在线双极性记忆。通过确定性分类器分配粗粒度失败类型，再结合混合词法-稠密检索器在冻结模型生成每次修订前进行条件检索。只有早期已完成的记忆中条目可被检索，避免数据泄露。

Result: 使用Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct，在相同初始预测和修复预算下，MERIT在Spider上将执行准确率从66.34%提升至69.79%，在BIRD上从47.35%提升至48.44%。配对分析证实Spider上的提升显著，但BIRD上的证据较弱。MERIT无法与无类型动态检索可靠区分，Reflexion式记忆在BIRD上达到51.24%但推理成本更高。

Conclusion: 消融实验显示负向记忆贡献有限，类型条件和词法-稠密排序的价值因数据集而异，schema内经验提供最一致的收益。研究澄清了因果跨查询记忆何时改善修复，以及何时更偏好更广泛的记忆表示。

Abstract: LLM agents that repair failures often discard successful corrections, forcing later episodes to rediscover similar solutions. We study whether finalized repair outcomes can improve subsequent Text-to-SQL episodes without parameter updates. We introduce MERIT, a training-free agent that maintains an online dual-polarity memory of oracle-verified corrections and observed unsuccessful directions. Under oracle-assisted benchmark feedback, only memories from earlier finalized episodes are eligible for retrieval. A deterministic classifier assigns a coarse failure type, which conditions a hybrid lexical-dense retriever before the frozen model generates each revision. Using Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct with identical initial predictions and repair budgets, MERIT improves execution accuracy over stateless iterative repair from \(66.34\%\) to \(69.79\%\) on Spider and from \(47.35\%\) to \(48.44\%\) on BIRD. Paired analyses provide clear evidence for the Spider gain but weaker evidence on BIRD. MERIT is not reliably separated from untyped dynamic retrieval on either benchmark, while Reflexion-style memory reaches \(51.24\%\) on BIRD at substantially higher inference cost. Ablations show that negative memory contributes modestly, the value of type conditioning and lexical--dense ranking is dataset dependent, and schema-local experience provides the most consistent benefit. These results clarify when causal cross-query memory improves repair and when broader memory representations remain preferable.

</details>


### [157] [Clinical Communication Processing with Models Trained on LLM-Generated Synthetic Data: A Structured Survey and Novel Application Case Studies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05993)
*Alexander Apartsin,Yehudit Aperstein*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文是一篇结构化的叙述性综述，探讨如何利用大语言模型将临床源数据（如病历、诊断标签、症状列表、护理计划）转化为合成的书面和转录沟通文本，用于训练下游临床NLP系统。综述按源表示、沟通形式和参与者、生成方法、下游任务四个维度组织，并辅以十三个新颖的案例研究，涵盖EMS院前报告、野战无线电伤员记录、护士交接、患者门户分诊及低资源出院沟通等场景，证明合成沟通可用于引导构建这些系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床价值大量蕴含在沟通交流中（患者描述症状、临床医生推理和下达指令、救护车与急诊交接、护士轮班交接等），而非结构化记录中。这类语言不同于表格数据，其含义依赖于说话者角色、意图、因果性、不确定性、省略和信道噪声。然而真实临床沟通语料稀缺，因为真实交流涉及隐私、碎片化且标注昂贵，这严重阻碍了医疗NLP在该领域的发展。

Method: 论文采用结构化叙述性综述方法，按四个维度组织文献：源表示、沟通形式与参与者、生成方法和下游任务。同时补充了十三个原创案例研究，这些案例针对没有标注真实数据的沟通渠道和语言构建临床NLP系统，涵盖EMS院前报告、野战无线电伤员记录、护士交接、患者门户分诊、低资源出院沟通等场景。

Result: 案例研究表明合成沟通可以引导构建临床NLP系统。主要发现包括：微调编码器模型优于评估的零样本基线；故意降级的沟通对于鲁棒性具有价值。主要局限是大多数研究在留出的合成沟通数据上评估，而"合成训练、真实测试"的证据仍然有限。

Conclusion: 合成临床沟通正在成为一种实用的研究资源；但要将其确立为可复用的临床基础设施，需要真实数据迁移、安全性和外部验证。

Abstract: Much clinical value is conveyed not through structured records but through communication: exchanges in which patients describe symptoms, clinicians reason and give instructions, ambulances hand over to emergency departments, and nurses pass on a shift. Such language differs from tabular data because meaning depends on speaker role, intent, causality, uncertainty, omission, and channel noise. Healthcare natural language processing must therefore interpret information as conveyed rather than coded. This requires well-annotated corpora, which are scarce because authentic exchanges are private, fragmented, and costly to annotate. Large language models offer a way forward by transforming clinical sources, such as records, diagnostic labels, symptom lists, or care plans, into written and transcribed communication for downstream models. We present a structured narrative survey organized by source representation, communication form and participants, generation method, and downstream task, complemented by thirteen novel case studies. These build clinical NLP systems for communication channels and languages without labeled real-world data, including EMS pre-arrival reports, field-radio casualty documentation, nurse handoffs, patient-portal triage, and low-resource discharge communication. They show that synthetic communication can bootstrap such systems. Findings include the competitiveness of fine-tuned encoder models over evaluated zero-shot baselines and the value of deliberately degraded communication for robustness. The main limitation is that most studies evaluate on held-out synthetic communication, while train-on-synthetic, test-on-authentic evidence remains limited. We conclude that syn-thetic clinical communication is becoming a practical research resource; establishing it as reusable clinical infrastructure will require authentic-data transfer, safety and external validation.

</details>


### [158] [EpiBench: Can LLMs Understand Epitopes for Antibody Drug Discovery?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06022)
*Zirui Wang,Jiaqi Wang,Qinghan Wang,Yuzhi Xu,Gang Du,Tingjun Hou,Odin Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出EpiBench，一个基于序列的、封闭式、可自动评分的基准，用于评估大语言模型在抗体表位推理方面的能力。EpiBench包含1,609个样本，涵盖五个关联任务，评估结果显示当前LLM在抗体特异性序列理解、长上下文残基定位和生物推理方面仍有局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表位决定抗体结合抗原的位置并影响功能阻断和逃逸抵抗等治疗特性，但现有LLM能否直接从抗原和抗体序列推断表位信息尚不明确。现有表位资源集中于孤立预测任务或依赖专门的结构设置，一般蛋白质基准也未评估抗体开发全流程中的表位决策。

Method: 构建EpiBench基准：包含1,609个基于结构抗体-抗原接触、功能性B细胞实验及深度突变扫描逃逸测量构建的样本；涵盖五个关联任务（可靶向区域发现、抗体条件表位识别、表位分箱、功能表位评估、抗体逃逸评估）；采用受控采样减少捷径评估伪影；评估九个通用LLM并通过任务基线、抗原长度分层、显式推理对比和失败模式检查分析其行为。

Result: 当前LLM能捕获部分表位相关信号，但在抗体特异性序列锚定、长上下文残基定位以及生物学推理方面仍存在显著局限。

Conclusion: EpiBench为衡量和改进序列感知的生物医学LLM提供了诊断性测试平台，推动可靠的LLM辅助抗体发现。

Abstract: Epitopes determine where antibodies bind antigens and shape downstream therapeutic properties such as functional blockade and escape resistance, making epitope understanding central to antibody drug discovery. Although large language models (LLMs) have shown strong biomedical reasoning ability, it remains unclear whether they can infer epitope information directly from antigen and antibody sequences. Existing epitope resources typically focus on isolated prediction tasks or rely on specialized structural settings, while general protein benchmarks do not evaluate epitope-centered decisions across the antibody development workflow. To address this gap, we introduce EpiBench, a closed-book, sequence-based, and automatically scorable benchmark for evaluating epitope reasoning in LLMs. EpiBench contains 1,609 curated samples grounded in structural antibody--antigen contacts, curated functional B-cell assays, and deep mutational scanning escape measurements. It covers five connected tasks: targetable region discovery, antibody-conditioned epitope identification, epitope binning, functional epitope assessment, and antibody escape assessment, with controlled sampling to reduce shortcut-based evaluation artifacts. We evaluate nine general-purpose LLMs and analyze their behavior through task-specific baselines, antigen length stratification, explicit-reasoning comparison, and failure-mode inspection. The results show that current LLMs capture partial epitope-related signals but remain limited in antibody-specific sequence grounding, long-context residue localization, and biologically grounded reasoning. Therefore, EpiBench provides a diagnostic testbed for measuring and improving sequence-aware biomedical LLMs toward reliable LLM-assisted antibody discovery.

</details>


### [159] [FormBharo: Designing and Evaluating a Voice Agent for Conversational Form Filling in Rural India](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06027)
*Aman Dalmia,Sanskriti Midha,Jigar Doshi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: FormBharo是一个多语言语音智能体系统，通过电话将结构化表单（如公共卫生项目的注册表格）自动填写完毕，并由LLM与确定性规则验证及流程控制相结合，在严格延迟和成本预算内工作。在印度ARMMAN NGO的产前/产后护理筛选项目中试点，并开源FormVoiceAgentBench基准以评估现实语音条件下的表单完成表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 印度的社会福利申请通常从填写表格开始，但最需要这些福利的人群往往无法读写，需要面对面口头交流。目前这项工作依赖一线健康工作者逐一登记受益人，效率低下且浪费有限的人手。因此需要一个能够通过电话语音对话自动填写结构化表单的语音智能体系统，在低成本、低延迟的前提下服务于不识字的低收入人群。

Method: 将大型语言模型（LLMs）与确定性的基于规则的验证和流程控制相结合，构建一个通过电话对话填写结构化表单的语音智能体。系统由语音转录、信息抽取、回复生成等模块组成，并配套发布了FormVoiceAgentBench基准，包含人类录制的印地语音频和3760个多轮对话测试（覆盖960个模拟通话），用于评估各个组件及端到端表单完成性能。

Result: 当LLM接收有错误的真实语音转录而非参考转录时，表单完成率下降最多约41个百分点。基于规则的流程控制能够恢复许多对话轮次的抽取错误，使较小的廉价模型在表单完成率上达到或超越前沿模型。组件性能无法预测端到端性能：GPT-5.5在参考转录上对话轮抽取准确率领先（99.8%），但在表单完成率上排名较低。由于错误在管道中既传播又相互抵消，最优模型选择只能通过端到端评估显现。没有单一模型在准确率、成本和延迟三方面同时最优，因此采用基于帕累托的加权求和标量化选择部署配置。

Conclusion: 端到端的表单完成评估优于组件级评估；规则控制的引入可以有效降低对最强（更昂贵）LLM的依赖；在真实部署场景中需通过帕累托优化在准确率、成本与延迟之间取得平衡。

Abstract: In India, almost every social benefit starts with a form, yet the people who need these benefits most are often unable to read or write. Reaching them requires a spoken conversation. Today that work falls to frontline health workers who enroll beneficiaries one at a time, a poor use of stretched capacity. We built FormBharo ("fill the form" in Hindi), a voice agent that fills a structured form over a phone call under tight latency and cost budgets by pairing Large Language Models (LLMs) with deterministic, rule-based validation and flow control. It is being piloted with ARMMAN, an NGO running large-scale maternal and child mobile-health programs in India, to enroll low-income, Hindi-speaking mothers in antenatal and postnatal care. To our knowledge, it is the first voice agent piloted to fill an enrollment form for this population. We openly release FormVoiceAgentBench, a benchmark pairing human-recorded Hindi audio with 3,760 multi-turn conversation tests across 960 simulated calls, to evaluate our agent's components (transcription, extraction, reply generation) and end-to-end form completion under real acoustic variations. Form completion drops by up to ~41 points when LLMs receive error-prone real-speech transcripts instead of reference ones. The rule-based controls recover many turn-level extraction errors, helping smaller, cheaper models match or surpass frontier models on form completion. Component performance does not predict end-to-end performance: GPT-5.5 leads turn-level extraction accuracy on reference transcripts (99.8%) but ranks lower on form completion. Since errors both propagate and cancel across the pipeline, the optimal model choice of models emerges only through end-to-end evaluation. Finally, no single model is best across accuracy, cost, and latency at once, so we use a Pareto-based weighted-sum scalarization to select a deployable configuration balancing the three.

</details>


### [160] [Training-Free Token-Level Steering for LLM Personalized Co-Writing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06069)
*Wenhao Mao,Chengbin Hou,Weixiao Wang,Jialiang Zhu,Min Liu,Yibin Hao,Hairong Lv*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SteerWrite是一个免训练的个性化协同写作框架，无需梯度更新即可将基础模型适配到专业领域，在小数据集上表现优异，并在多个数据集和模型上达到最先进性能，显著减少人工编辑工作量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在个性化任务中缺乏专业领域知识；微调方法计算成本高且难以应对快速数据更新；检索增强生成无法提供细粒度的token级引导；现有界面以聊天为主，缺乏编码领域之外的生产力协同写作范式。

Method: SteerWrite采用免训练框架实现个性化协同写作，无需梯度更新即可适配专业领域，并针对小数据集设计了特定的适配方案，实现token级的细粒度引导。

Result: 在多个数据集、指标和模型上达到最先进的性能，显著减少了人工编辑工作量。

Conclusion: SteerWrite证明了在无需微调的情况下，通过免训练方法可以有效实现个性化协同写作，兼顾领域适配性、计算效率和细粒度控制。

Abstract: While Large Language Models (LLMs) show great promise for personalization, they often lack specialized domain knowledge. Conventional solutions like fine-tuning struggle with high computational costs and rapid data updates, while Retrieval-Augmented Generation fails to provide fine-grained, token-level steering. Furthermore, chat-based interfaces remain dominant, whereas productive co-writing paradigms have not yet been well exploited beyond the coding domain. To this end, we introduce SteerWrite, a training-free framework designed for personalized co-writing. Our method effectively adapts the base model to specialized domains without gradient updates, with specific designs tailored to small datasets. Experiments demonstrate that SteerWrite achieves state-of-the-art performance across diverse datasets, metrics, and models, significantly reducing human editing effort.

</details>


### [161] [Beyond Sequence Order: Syntax-Informed Positional Embeddings for Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06111)
*Haris Riaz,Hyungji Kim,Mihai Surdeanu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出SiPE（语法感知位置嵌入），通过在预训练期间从依存句法分析中学习轻量级语法先验，并将其注入三类主流位置嵌入（绝对、相对、旋转）中。研究发现注入位置取决于架构：自回归解码器在相对PE中乘法耦合注意力分数效果最佳，编码器则直接加到输入嵌入中。SiPE在SyntaxGym基准上提升最高10.3%，同时降低困惑度9.0%，并在GLUE上提升8.2%，且推理时仅需单次句法分析，建立了语法监督与推理成本之间的新帕累托前沿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer的位置嵌入主要编码token距离和顺序，但对句法结构几乎不感知。现有语法注入方法要么在推理时对多个句法树做边缘化（计算成本高），要么在运行时丢弃语法信息，缺乏高效且有效的语法感知方案。作者旨在探索如何以轻量、高效的方式将句法先验注入不同架构（编码器/解码器）和不同位置嵌入家族中。

Method: SiPE在预训练阶段从依存句法分析中学习一个轻量级语法先验，并系统性地测试该先验在不同架构（编码器与解码器）和不同位置嵌入家族（绝对、相对、旋转）中的最佳注入方式：对于使用相对PE的自回归解码器，先验应以乘法方式与注意力分数的相对位置项耦合；对于编码器，先验应直接加到输入嵌入中，与原生位置机制组合。除位置嵌入注入外，self-attention和其他架构部分保持不变。推理时仅需一次句法分析。

Result: SiPE在SyntaxGym基准上比无语法监督的基线提升最高10.3%，同时降低困惑度9.0%（而几乎所有现有语法注入方法都会在困惑度上退化）。在GLUE基准上提升最高8.2%，证明其收益超越句法泛化，扩展到真实语言理解任务。此外，SiPE仅在推理时使用单次句法分析，优于需要多句法树边缘化的现有方法。

Conclusion: SiPE证明了一种轻量、高效的语法感知位置嵌入方案：通过在预训练中注入依存句法先验，不仅能提升句法泛化能力，还能改善通用语言理解，且不会牺牲推理效率。研究还揭示了语法先验注入位置与架构/PE类型的依赖关系，为后续语法增强Transformer设计提供了指导原则。

Abstract: Positional embeddings (PE) in Transformers encode token distance and order but are largely agnostic to \textit{syntactic structure}. We introduce \textbf{S}yntax-\textbf{i}nformed \textbf{P}ositional \textbf{E}mbeddings (\textbf{SiPE}), which learns a lightweight syntactic prior from dependency parses during pretraining and injects it across all three dominant PE families (absolute, relative, rotary), for both encoders and decoders, leaving self-attention and the rest of the architecture untouched. We isolate \emph{where} and \emph{how} the prior should enter the model, and find it depends on the architecture: for autoregressive decoders that use relative PE, the prior is strongest when coupled multiplicatively with the relative-position term of the attention score, outperforming injection into the input embeddings, into self-attention, or into the positional and attention terms jointly---while for encoders it is best added directly to the input embeddings, composing with each encoder's native positional mechanism. We find that models pre-trained with SiPE improve on the SyntaxGym benchmark by up to $10.3\%$ while simultaneously reducing perplexity by $9.0\%$ over a base model with no syntactic supervision---a metric nearly every existing syntax-injection method instead degrades. Crucially, these gains extend beyond syntactic generalization: SiPE also improves real-world language understanding, raising scores on the GLUE benchmark by up to $8.2\%$ over a model trained without it. Unlike existing syntactic language models that marginalize over many parses at inference or discard syntax at runtime, SiPE conditions on a single parse, establishing a new Pareto frontier between syntactic supervision and inference cost.

</details>


### [162] [Decolonizing Linguistic Policies in Automated Speech Recognition: A Framework for Cross-Culturally Competent Speech AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06141)
*Jay L. Cunningham,Mark Atta Mensah,Richard Martinez,Joao Vieira da Silva Neto,Efi Dawodu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文探讨自动语音识别（ASR）系统中对低资源、原住民和非标准语言变体的持续性识别失败，认为这不仅是技术错误，更是隐性的语言政策，再现了殖民语言层级。作者提出"三伤害"（3M）分类法（误认、错位、不信任）和一个七层情境化模型，并倡导参与式框架和最小审计协议，使受影响社区成为共同设计者、评估者和治理伙伴。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ASR系统在公共服务的普及使其语言处理能力具有重要社会影响，但对低资源、原住民和非标准语言变体的系统性失败被当作纯技术问题处理，未认识到其背后隐含的权力结构和殖民语言等级制度。

Method: 整合语言资本、种族语言学意识形态、语言政策研究和去殖民计算等理论框架，提出3M（Misrecognition, Misalignment, Mistrust）伤害分类法及七层情境化模型，并设计参与式框架与最小审计协议。

Result: 提出了一套分析ASR语言不公的理论工具（3M分类法与七层模型），以及面向实践的文化胜任审计与参与式治理方案。

Conclusion: ASR的语言失败应被视为隐性的语言政策问题，需将受影响社区置于设计、评估与治理的核心位置，以实现技术与语言正义的融合。

Abstract: This paper focuses on automatic speech recognition (ASR) and ASR-mediated voice interfaces that shape access to public services, healthcare, and education. We argue that persistent failures for low-resource, Indigenous, and non-standard language varieties are not only technical errors, but also implicit linguistic policies that reproduce colonial language hierarchies. Drawing on linguistic capital, raciolinguistic ideology, language policy research, and decolonial computing, we show how data, metrics, and model priors determine whose voices become machine-legible. We introduce the Three Harms (3M) taxonomy---Misrecognition, Misalignment, and Mistrust---and a seven-layer situatedness model for linguistic diversity in ASR and ASR-mediated voice interfaces. We then propose a participatory framework and minimum audit protocol for culturally competent ASR, positioning affected communities as co-designers, evaluators, and governance partners.

</details>


### [163] [Routing Is Least Learnable Where It Is Most Valuable: Bounds on Representation Routing for Web Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06171)
*Jiaming Wei,Zekun Wu,Adriano Koshiyama,Maria Perez-Ortiz*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统测量了Web代理在六种观察模式（文本、像素或两者结合）下的表现，跨越8个站点-模型组合。研究发现各模式互补且失败方式不同，但"预言机"收益被运行噪声夸大（重跑同一模式可改变12-14%结果）。固定的最优模式选择与复杂路由策略相当，唯一稳健的收益是成本控制。核心障碍是路由监督信号受限于代理自身成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Web代理的观察模式（文本、像素或混合）通常固定不变，但不同任务可能需要不同模式。作者想量化按任务动态选择观察模式的潜在收益，检验"一个模式走天下"假设是否成立。

Method: 在WebArena和VisualWebArena上测量六种观察模式×8个站点-模型组合的完整矩阵；通过重跑实验量化噪声；设计五种路由策略（模式选择、强模式花费决策、零成本文本规则、置信度级联、分池成本层）并进行对比测试；分析路由监督信号与代理能力之间的关系。

Result: 模式互补且最优选择随任务集反转；预言机收益被噪声膨胀（重跑改变12-14%结果）；成本受限策略在8/8单元格中节省9.5-30.6%成本且成功率不变；五种路由策略无一种稳健超越固定最优模式；路由监督与代理成功率相关（相关性0.95）。

Conclusion: 当前结论对固定单模式选择有利，路由的局限性源于当前代理能力不足而非路由本身。更强代理会同时获得更多标签和路由机会，可改变结论。作者提供了完整测量协议和噪声带供后续研究参考。

Abstract: Web agents observe a browser through text, pixels, or both, and the choice is usually fixed once for all tasks. We measure six observation modes across eight site-model combinations (cells) on VisualWebArena and WebArena and ask what choosing per task would buy. The modes are complementary: each solves tasks the others miss, they fail in structurally different ways, and the best choice reverses between task sets. The obvious prize, an oracle that picks a winning mode for every task, looks large but is inflated by run-to-run noise: rerunning the same mode on the same tasks changes 12-14% of outcomes, so a second run of a mode already in hand gains about as much as adding a new one. What survives is a cost bound: sending only the tasks no mode solves to the cheapest mode cuts cost by 9.5-30.6% in 8 of 8 cells at unchanged success. We then test five routing policies (picking the mode, deciding when to spend on the strong mode, a zero-cost rule read off the task text, a confidence cascade, and pooled cost tiers), and none robustly beats simply fixing one well-chosen mode; the one exception is a fragile result in our sparsest cell. The central obstruction is that routing supervision is produced at the agent's success rate: the weaker the agent, the fewer labels a router gets, exactly where routing would be most valuable. This limit belongs to today's agents rather than to routing itself. Label supply and routing opportunity rise together (correlation 0.95 across cells), so a stronger agent can overturn the result, and we report the rerun noise bands and the full measurement protocol.

</details>


### [164] [NeSy-RAG: Neuro-Symbolic RAG for Explainable Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06292)
*Jonas Gann,Michael Gertz*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: NeSy-RAG是一个模块化的神经符号检索增强生成框架，通过将检索到的文本块合成可溯源的Prolog模块，实现确定性的推理答案和透明的执行轨迹，并引入符号知识缺口检测机制来识别缺失的用户事实。在ShARC基准上无需领域特定训练即达到61.1%准确率，显著优于同模型RAG基线的42.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统RAG存在推理过程不透明、中间步骤难以验证和归因的问题，且用户特定上下文缺失时缺乏系统性检测，常导致不完整或错误的输出。

Method: 1) 从检索文本块生成具有语义意义的谓词，编码布尔声明；2) 利用联合自然语言-代码嵌入检索并组合谓词为Prolog查询；3) 引入符号知识缺口检测机制，识别影响查询结果的缺失用户事实并触发交互；4) 执行Prolog查询产生确定性答案和透明执行轨迹。

Result: 在ShARC基准上，无需领域特定训练，NeSy-RAG达到61.1%准确率，远超同模型RAG基线的42.8%准确率。

Conclusion: NeSy-RAG通过神经符号结合的方式有效提升了RAG的推理可解释性和答案准确性，其符号知识缺口检测机制能系统性地发现缺失的上下文信息。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) improves question answering by grounding large language models (LLMs) in external knowledge such as text corpora. However, its reasoning process remains largely opaque: intermediate reasoning steps are difficult to verify and cannot be reliably attributed to specific evidence. Moreover, missing user-specific context is rarely detected systematically, often leading to incomplete or incorrect output.
  We propose NeSy-RAG, a modular neuro-symbolic RAG framework that synthesizes attributable Prolog modules from retrieved text chunks. For each chunk, the system generates semantically meaningful predicates that encode Boolean claims, which may depend on user facts. Using joint natural language-code embeddings, predicates are retrieved and composed into Prolog queries. To address incomplete user context, we introduce a symbolic knowledge-gap detection mechanism that identifies missing user facts whose truth values affect the query outcome and automatically triggers follow-up interactions.
  Executing the resulting Prolog queries yields deterministic answers together with transparent execution traces that link each reasoning step to its originating source. On the ShARC benchmark, without domain-specific training, NeSy-RAG achieves 61.1% accuracy, outperforming a same-model RAG baseline that achieves 42.8% accuracy.

</details>


### [165] [Benchmarking and Enhancing LLMs for Rule-Intensive Review of National Standard Documents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06312)
*Tao Wang,Qihao Yang,Rongjiao Liang,Lianghong Lin,Haitao Wang,Xinyu Cao,Tianyong Hao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出GB/T-Bench，这是首个面向国家标准文档结构化审查的基准，包含分级审查分类法（25种可诊断错误类型）、可控反例生成机制（488篇文档生成7,306个可追踪错误实例）以及面向诊断的评估协议。同时提出GB/T-Reviewer多智能体框架，实验显示最强模型CMCS仅0.3280（专家0.6640），而GB/T-Reviewer提升至0.5094，揭示了规则密集型文档审查中人机差距与结构化技能协同的价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在规则密集型文档审查（如国家标准文档）中的能力缺乏系统评估。现有基准聚焦于领域知识和问答，忽略了专业文档的内在质量审查，而这类审查依赖人工专家，成本高且难以规模化。

Method: 构建GB/T-Bench基准：(1)提出GB/T审查分类法，涵盖文档结构、范围对齐、规范性语态、术语一致性和规范性引用五个维度，含25种可诊断错误类型；(2)设计可控反例生成机制，结合确定性规则与受约束的LLM改写，将488篇文档转化为7,306个可追踪错误实例；(3)开发面向诊断的评估协议，要求错误位置、审查维度和错误类型精确匹配，并引入文档级覆盖率指标；(4)提出GB/T-Reviewer多智能体框架，将审查知识转化为专业技能，协调全局检查、定向诊断、规则扫描和结果验证。

Result: 对14个主流LLM的实验表明：最强模型CMCS仅0.3280，而专家达到0.6640，存在显著人机差距。GB/T-Reviewer将最佳CMCS提升至0.5094，验证了结构化技能协同对规则密集型文档审查的价值。

Conclusion: 该工作揭示了LLM在国家标准文档规则审查上的不足，证明了多智能体技能协同可显著提升审查能力，为标准化及高风险文档领域的可信AI铺平道路。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) increasingly support complex professional tasks, yet their capabilities in rule-intensive document review remain insufficiently evaluated. National standard documents, such as China GB/T standards, offer a representative testbed: they are lengthy, highly structured, and governed by explicit rules for scope, terminology, normative wording, and cross-section consistency. Existing benchmarks focus on domain knowledge and question answering, largely overlooking intrinsic quality review for professional documents. Such reviews rely heavily on human experts, making them costly and difficult to scale. To bridge this gap, we introduce GB/T-Bench, the first benchmark for the structured review of national standard documents. Its GB/T Review Taxonomy is a hierarchical schema covering document structure, scope alignment, normative modality, terminology consistency, and normative references, with 25 diagnosable error types. A controllable counterexample generation mechanism combines deterministic rules and constrained LLM rewriting to process 488 documents into 7,306 traceable review error instances for evaluation. We also develop a diagnosis-oriented evaluation protocol requiring exact matches on error location, review dimension, and error type, plus document-level coverage metrics. We further propose GB/T-Reviewer, a multi-agent framework that converts review knowledge into specialized skills and coordinates global inspection, targeted diagnosis, rule scanning, and result verification. Experiments with 14 mainstream LLMs reveal a substantial human-LLM gap: the strongest model achieves only 0.3280 CMCS versus 0.6640 for experts. GB/T-Reviewer raises the best CMCS to 0.5094, showing the value of structured skill coordination for rule-intensive document review. This work paves the way for trustworthy AI in standardization and other high-stakes document domains.

</details>


### [166] [Benchmarking the Benchmarks: Evaluating Benchmarks for Conversational Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06329)
*Noam Koren,Roy Bar-Haim,Abigail Goldsteen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于LLM裁判的无参考框架，用于评估面向任务的对话智能体基准的质量，涵盖一致性、复杂性和策略覆盖率三个维度，并提供可操作的诊断建议。实验验证了该框架与人工标注的一致性，并能有效区分不同质量的基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的面向任务对话智能体基准通常通过人工策划或自动生成，但基准本身的质量很少被评估。低质量的基准可能包含不一致的任务、过于简化的场景或有限的策略覆盖，导致评估结果不可靠。因此需要一种系统性的基准质量评估方法。

Method: 提出一个参考自由的框架，利用LLM作为裁判来评估基准的三个关键维度：一致性（任务内部是否矛盾）、复杂度（场景难度和丰富度）和策略覆盖率（策略空间的覆盖程度），同时提供针对弱点的可操作诊断信息。

Result: 框架与独立人工标注结果具有一致性；能够区分不同能力LLM生成的基准质量；能识别受到受控质量劣化扰动影响的基准；在多个领域和裁判模型下均能稳定区分基准质量等级；并成功应用于人工策划的基准。

Conclusion: 该框架为评估合成和人工策划的对话智能体基准提供了一种实用方法，填补了基准质量评估的空白，有助于构建更可靠的对话智能体评估体系。

Abstract: Task-oriented conversational agents are evaluated using curated or automatically generated benchmarks, yet benchmark quality is rarely assessed. Poor benchmarks may contain inconsistent tasks, simplistic scenarios, or limited policy coverage, leading to unreliable evaluations. We introduce a reference-free framework that uses LLM judges to assess benchmark consistency, complexity, and policy coverage, while providing actionable diagnostics of weaknesses. We validate the framework by demonstrating agreement with independent human annotations and by evaluating benchmarks generated by LLMs of varying capabilities, as well as benchmarks subjected to controlled quality-degrading perturbations. Across domains and judge models, the proposed metrics consistently distinguish between benchmark quality levels. We further demonstrate the framework's applicability to manually curated benchmarks. Our framework offers a practical approach for evaluating synthetic and manually curated conversational-agent benchmarks.

</details>


### [167] [RP-OPSD: Reasoning-Pivot-Guided On-Policy Self-Distillation for Multilingual Reasoning Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06347)
*Xinye Wang,Junxiao Liu,Shujian Huang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出RP-OPSD方法，通过识别目标语言推理中的"推理枢轴"（reasoning pivots）token，利用有/无英文参照的教师视图分布差异作为代理信号，对这些关键推理决策进行特权蒸馏和参考锚定，从而提升LLMs在多种语言上的数学推理迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多语言推理迁移对将LLM的推理能力扩展到高资源语言之外至关重要。现有的OPSD及其变体虽提供密集的token级监督，但其目标函数并未明确优先考虑对跨语言迁移最关键的推理信号。作者认识到目标语言推理同时包含"表面文本生成"和"推理枢轴"（推进或改变推理方向的决策），而现有方法未能区分这两类token的重要性。

Method: 提出RP-OPSD（Reasoning-Pivot-guided On-Policy Self-Distillation）框架：1）将有/无英文参照答案的匹配教师视图之间的分布偏移作为推理枢轴的操作性代理指标；2）基于该代理指标引导特权蒸馏（privileged distillation），将训练资源集中在推理枢轴token上；3）同时引入参考锚定（reference anchoring）机制进一步强化关键推理决策的学习。

Result: 在覆盖17种语言和多难度级别的数学推理基准上，RP-OPSD优于强多语言推理基线和OPSD变体。进一步分析表明，该方法成功地将特权蒸馏集中在推理控制和基于问题条件的状态更新token上，同时降低了对主要支持表面实现的token的关注度。

Conclusion: 通过聚焦推理枢轴而非均匀处理所有token，RP-OPSD有效改善了多语言推理迁移的质量。该方法提供了可操作的思路：识别并优先训练对推理进程起关键控制作用的token，而非在表面文本生成上浪费蒸馏资源。代码已开源。

Abstract: Multilingual reasoning transfer is crucial for extending reasoning capabilities of large language models (LLMs) beyond high-resource languages. On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) and its variants have emerged as a promising paradigm, providing dense token-level supervision on student-generated rollouts, yet their objectives do not explicitly prioritize reasoning signals most critical to cross-lingual transfer. We characterize that target-language reasoning comprises the generation of both surface text and reasoning pivots, which are decisions that advance or redirect the reasoning process and shape subsequent inference. This motivates concentrating privileged distillation around such pivots. We therefore propose RP-OPSD, Reasoning-Pivot-guided On-Policy Self-Distillation, using the distributional shift between matched teacher views with and without an English reference solution as an operational proxy to guide privileged distillation and reference anchoring. Experiments on mathematical reasoning benchmarks covering 17 languages and multiple difficulty levels show that our method outperforms strong multilingual reasoning baselines and OPSD variants. Further analysis reveals that RP-OPSD concentrates privileged distillation on reasoning-control and problem-condistioned state-update tokens, while downweighting it for tokens that mainly support surface realization. Our code is available at https://github.com/NJUNLP/RP-OPSD.

</details>


### [168] [The Bitter Lesson of Tool Calling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06370)
*Ishan Patel,Sahil Sen,Elias Lumer,Vamse Kumar Subbiah*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文在BFCL v4基准上系统比较了程序化工具调用(PTC)与原生JSON工具调用在14个语言模型上的表现。PTC在11/14个模型中匹配或超越JSON基线(GPT-5.6家族提升10.6%)，在并行扇出场景下13/14个模型表现更优，且在上下文腐烂条件下保持稳定。结果表明PTC是JSON工具调用的可行且稳健的替代方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前缺乏在既有基准上、跨当前及先前模型代际、真实任务条件下，对"工具即代码"(程序化工具调用)进行系统评估的工作。作者希望填补这一空白，验证PTC范式是否优于标准的JSON工具调用。

Method: 在BFCL v4基准上，对14个语言模型进行程序化工具调用与原生JSON工具调用的实证对比。PTC范式将工具暴露为类型化Python桩代码，模型通过代码调用工具，执行与结果回传在单个代理回合内完成。测试覆盖标准场景、并行扇出(fan-out)场景及上下文腐烂(context rot)条件。

Result: PTC在11/14个模型中匹配或超越JSON基线；GPT-5.6家族相对JSON基线提升10.6%；并行扇出条件下13/14个模型达到或超越基线；上下文腐烂条件下PTC保持稳定，而基线平均退化2.3%。

Conclusion: 程序化工具调用是JSON工具调用的可行且稳健的替代方案，其性能与模型能力随发布代际一致增长，并在鲁棒性(并行、上下文腐烂)方面表现更优。

Abstract: Tool use transforms LLMs into agents that act beyond their training data, and for code-capable models, programmatic tool calling extends this further by replacing rigid JSON calls with scripts that chain and parallelize naturally. However, a systematic evaluation of tools as code on an established benchmark across current and prior model generations under real-world task conditions has not been conducted. In this work, we empirically compare programmatic tool calling (PTC) to native JSON tool calling across 14 language models on BFCL v4. In the programmatic tool calling paradigm, tools are exposed as typed Python stubs that the model invokes through code, with execution and results handled in a single agent turn. Programmatic tool calling matches or exceeds native JSON tool calling in 11 of 14 models on BFCL v4, with the GPT-5.6 family achieving a 10.6% improvement over the JSON tool calling baseline. Further, it matches or outperforms baseline in 13 of 14 models under parallel fan-out, and holds stable under context rot conditions where baseline degrades 2.3% on average. Our results demonstrate that programmatic tool calling is a viable and robust alternative to JSON tool calling, with performance tracking model capability across release generations.

</details>


### [169] [Learning When to Trust via Selective Context Preference Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06377)
*Xian Sun,Wei Chow,Yingshuo Wang,Junhao Liu,Wei Gao,Qing Wu,Lingdong Kong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出选择性信任（selective trust）框架来替代"抵抗误导信号"的单一目标，引入MIST基准（四种匹配条件下的人类标注推理数据集）和SC2W配对指标（衡量误导信号将正确答案翻转为错误的频率），并提出SCOPE方法（挖掘干净正确/误导错误样本，在四种条件均衡的配对偏好上优化DPO目标），在降低SC2W的同时保持上下文可信时的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型依赖外部信号作答时，一个误导信号就能让正确答案变错。简单的"训练模型抵抗误导"方案存在缺陷：完全忽略上下文的模型看似鲁棒，但在上下文值得信任时却毫无用处。因此需要将问题重新定义为选择性信任——模型应能区分何时该信、何时该拒，而非一味抵抗或不抵抗。

Method: 1) 构建MIST基准：对每个推理条目在四种匹配条件下（干净、误导、正确上下文、无关上下文）进行人类标注；2) 提出SC2W配对指标：统计误导信号将干净正确答案翻转为错误答案的频率；3) 提出SCOPE方法：挖掘干净正确/误导错误的失败样本，在四种条件均衡配对的偏好对上优化标准DPO目标，而非仅针对误导样本。

Result: 在广泛的基准研究中发现误导易感性是普遍存在的（universal）。SCOPE方法在流行开源模型上显著降低SC2W指标，同时在上下文为干净、正确或无关时保持原有的准确性。

Conclusion: 模型的评估标准应基于选择性信任（selective trust）而非单纯的抵抗能力（resistance alone）。选择性信任框架为语言模型的上下文可靠性评估提供了更细致、更实用的视角。

Abstract: Language models increasingly condition their answers on external signals, and a single misleading one can turn a correct answer wrong. The obvious remedy, training models to resist such signals, hides a failure mode: a model that ignores all context looks robust yet is useless when the context is worth trusting. We recast the problem as selective trust and introduce MIST, a human-annotated benchmark that renders each reasoning item under four matched conditions (clean, misleading, correct-context, and irrelevant-context), together with SC2W, a paired metric counting how often a misleading signal flips a clean-correct answer to wrong. Across a comprehensive benchmark study, we observe that such a susceptibility is universal. We then propose SCOPE, which mines clean-correct/misleading-wrong failures and optimizes a standard Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) objective over matched preference pairs balanced equally across all four conditions, rather than over misleading items alone. Our approach substantially reduces SC2W on popular open-sourced models while preserving accuracy when the added context is clean, correct, or irrelevant. With this work, we argue that models should be judged on selective trust, not on resistance alone.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [170] [MS-MLB: An Open Machine Learning Benchmark for Blood-Based MS Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05196)
*Adam Simson,Ankush Dutta,Quang Bui*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了MS-MLB（多发性硬化机器学习基准），一个基于GSE17048全血RNA表达数据的可复现开放基准，用于MS与健康对照的分类研究，采用防泄漏的评估流程（嵌套交叉验证、独立留出集、Bootstrap置信区间等），最终梯度提升算法在留出集上取得最优MS研究评分（93.83），并提供了文档化的外部模型提交通路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多发性硬化（MS）的诊断依赖临床评估、MRI和实验室证据，但血液RNA表达数据可能携带疾病相关的免疫信号。然而，目前缺乏一个可复现、标准化的机器学习基准来公平评估基于血液转录组的MS分类模型，且已有研究流程各异、难以比较。

Method: 使用公开的GSE17048队列，构建MS与健康对照的二分类任务；在共享且防泄漏的控制流程下评估多种机器学习算法，包括嵌套交叉验证、独立分层留出集、Bootstrap置信区间、ROC与PR分析、校准度量，并提出探索性的MS研究评分（MS Research Score）；框架内置文档化的外部模型提交通路。

Result: 在最终基准总结中，梯度提升（Gradient Boosting）在留出集上排名第一，MS研究评分为93.83，AUC-ROC为0.989，灵敏度0.950，特异度0.778，F1分数0.927，Brier分数0.050。

Conclusion: MS-MLB是首个专注于GSE17048全血RNA表达数据上MS与健康对照分类的开放基准，提供了可复现的评估框架和外部模型提交机制。该评分仅供研究比较用途，未经临床验证，不能替代临床诊断。

Abstract: Multiple sclerosis (MS) is diagnosed through clinical assessment, magnetic resonance imaging, laboratory evidence when appropriate, and exclusion of better explanations. Blood RNA expression data may contain disease associated immune signal, but a blood RNA classifier cannot be treated as a replacement for clinical diagnosis. This paper presents MS-MLB (Multiple Sclerosis Machine Learning Benchmark), a reproducible open benchmark for machine learning based MS research classification from whole blood RNA expression data. MS-MLB uses the public GSE17048 cohort, converts it into an MS versus healthy control task, and evaluates multiple algorithms under a shared, leakage controlled pipeline that a researcher can rerun without reconfiguring the evaluation. The evaluation includes nested cross-validation, an untouched stratified holdout set, bootstrap confidence intervals, ROC and precision recall analysis, calibration measurement, and an exploratory MS Research Score. In the final benchmark summary, Gradient Boosting ranked first by MS Research Score on the holdout set, with an MS Research Score of 93.83, AUC-ROC of 0.989, sensitivity of 0.950, specificity of 0.778, $F_{1}$ score of 0.927, and Brier score of 0.050. Prior studies have applied machine learning to MS blood transcriptomic data, including PBMC stage classification and whole blood diagnostic signature modeling. The contribution here is different and narrower. To our knowledge, MS-MLB is the first open benchmark focused on MS versus healthy control classification from GSE17048 whole blood RNA expression data with a documented external model submission pathway built into the framework. The score is intended for research comparison only and has not been clinically validated. The benchmark is accessible here: https://github.com/duckyquang/MS-MLB.

</details>


### [171] [When Do Corrective Features Help? An Agent for Corrective Feature Discovery on Black-Box Forecasters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05207)
*Fangxin Wang,Ziyi Zhang,Diyi Zhuang,Langzhou He,Shiyu Wang,Baichuan Mo,Philip S. Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CRAFTER是一种矫正性特征发现方法，通过挖掘冻结预测模型残差中的可解释特征来驱动轻量级后置矫正器，在六个数据集和六个冻结骨干网络上超越了所有专用特征工程系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 冻结的预训练预测器常以结构化、可重复的方式失败，而通过微调修复成本高昂。现有自动化特征工程建模的是数据生成过程，而非模型失败过程，因此需要一种矫正性特征发现方法来建模模型自身的失败模式。

Method: CRAFTER保持骨干网络冻结，通过两个互补生成器挖掘残差：(1)对原始输入通道的组合搜索；(2)由LLM提出命名特征组合、二元标志和简短可执行代码。所有候选特征通过单一的验证集门控机制接受或拒绝，最后由验证集选出的矫正器应用被接受的特征或保持预测不变。这种源无关的流水线允许在相同条件下评估之前的特征工程系统。

Result: 在六个公开数据集和六个冻结骨干网络上，CRAFTER在每种特征预算下都超越了所有专用特征工程系统，将矫正器单独实现的改进大约翻倍，并使最弱骨干网络的误差降低最多27%。这些增益在不同LLM后端下稳健，且在微调骨干网络上依然保持。

Conclusion: CRAFTER证明矫正性特征发现是冻结预测模型的有效修复手段，其源无关的流水线也为特征来源归因提供了可靠工具，未来可进一步探索更多样化的特征生成来源和更广泛的模型架构。

Abstract: Frozen pretrained forecasters often fail in structured, recurring ways that are costly to repair through fine-tuning. We study corrective feature discovery: mining interpretable features of a frozen forecaster's residual to drive a lightweight post-hoc corrector. Prior automated feature engineering models the data-generating process; corrective features instead model the model-failure process. We present CRAFTER (Corrective Residual Agent with Feature-based Temporal Exploration and Reasoning), which keeps the backbone frozen and mines its residual with two complementary generators: a compositional search over the raw input channels, and a large language model (LLM) that proposes named feature combinations, binary flags, and short executable code. A single validation-grounded gate accepts or rejects every candidate regardless of its origin, and a validation-selected corrector applies the accepted features or leaves the forecast unchanged. This source-agnostic pipeline also allows prior feature-engineering systems to be evaluated under identical conditions, making CRAFTER an instrument for attributing forecast improvements to the feature source alone. Across six public datasets and six frozen backbones, CRAFTER surpasses every dedicated feature-engineering system at every feature budget, roughly doubling the improvement achieved by the corrector alone and reducing the error of the weakest backbones by up to 27%. These gains are robust across different LLM backends and persist even when applied on top of fine-tuned backbones.

</details>


### [172] [PPDL: LLM-Based Flows as Probabilistic Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05234)
*Louis Mandel,Guillaume Baudart,Mandana Vaziri,Martin Hirzel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于编程基于LLM流程的概率语言，使开发者能够量化和传播整个应用流程中的不确定性，并在无需修改流程逻辑代码的情况下尝试不同的推理扩展技术。论文通过实验研究和Rocq定理证明器的案例研究验证了该能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建基于LLM的可靠应用仍然是一个重大挑战。LLM输出往往缺乏准确性和置信度度量，这种不确定性在多个LLM调用和其他工具组成的流程中会累积放大，使开发者和最终用户难以信任结果。

Method: 提出一种概率语言（probabilistic language）用于编程LLM流程，支持在流程层面量化与传播不确定性，并允许开发者通过声明式方式实验不同的推理扩展（inference scaling）技术，无需添加额外代码。

Result: 通过实验研究展示了该语言的概率量化与传播能力，并通过构建Rocq定理证明器智能体的案例研究验证了其在实际场景中的有效性。

Conclusion: 该概率语言为LLM流程提供了一种系统化的不确定性管理方案，有助于提升基于LLM应用的可靠性，同时降低了推理扩展技术实验的成本。

Abstract: Building reliable applications that leverage large language models (LLMs) remains a significant challenge. While LLMs offer impressive capabilities across diverse tasks, their outputs often lack accuracy and provide no clear measure of confidence. This uncertainty compounds in flows of multiple calls to LLMs and other tools, making it difficult for developers and end-users to trust the results. This paper introduces a probabilistic language for programming LLM-based flows. It enables developers to quantify and propagate uncertainty throughout the application's flow, and experiment with different inference scaling techniques without adding a single line of code beyond the flow's logic. We present an experimental study to demonstrate this capability, and a case study building a theorem proving agent for the Rocq theorem prover.

</details>


### [173] [Decoupling Perception from Description: Computation-Grounded Representation Alignment between Multivariate Time Series and Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05238)
*Xinran Feng,Yi Xie,Chao Zhang,Ruikun Li,Wanyun Ling,Ziyue Li,Chenxi Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CGTime，一种4B参数的时间序列-语言多模态模型。核心思想是解耦感知与描述：用确定性代码从真实多元时间序列中计算统计量，再由LLM将这些预计算的事实转化为语言描述，从而绕过了"自监督陷阱"（标签质量受限于标注模型感知能力）和单变量数据局限的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有训练多模态模型对齐时间序列与语言的方法存在两个关键问题：(1) 自监督陷阱——让LLM读序列写描述，标签质量受限于模型自身的感知能力，数据无法教会模型超出标注者已知的内容；(2) 大多数数据集只用单变量，但真正重要的模式（跨通道相关、领先-滞后结构、共现异常）只在多变量中出现。这导致"三元悖论"：现有方法要么可靠、要么真实、要么可扩展，无法三者兼得。

Method: 将感知与描述解耦。具体而言，使用确定性代码从真实开源多元时间序列中计算统计量集合，然后让LLM将这些预计算的事实用自然语言表述出来。感知（LLM不擅长的部分）由计算处理，而表达（描述）由LLM负责。由此构建CGTime（4B参数）计算接地的时间序列-语言模型。

Result: CGTime在多元理解任务上超越远大于它的通用模型：在保留基准上取得最佳多元事实分数（0.283），对比GPT-4o-mini（0.173）和GPT-5.4-nano（0.203）。该优势通过了针对所有基线的Holm校正配对显著性检验。此外，生成的描述中包含的可验证数值事实更准确，覆盖的统计属性范围更广。

Conclusion: 通过将感知任务外包给确定性计算、将表述任务交给LLM，成功解决了时间序列-语言对齐中的自监督陷阱和多变量数据缺失问题，以较小的模型规模实现了更强的多元时间序列理解能力。

Abstract: Training multimodal models to align time series with language runs into a self-supervision trap. The usual recipe asks an LLM to read a series and write a description, so label quality is capped by the perceptual skill the model is supposed to learn. The data can never teach more than the labeler already knows. A second gap makes this worse: most datasets use a single variable, but the patterns that matter (cross-channel correlation, lead-lag structure, co-occurring anomalies) appear only with several variables, right where the labeling LLM's limits are most exposed. These two problems create a trilemma: existing methods are reliable, realistic, or scalable, but none achieves all three. We resolve this by decoupling perception from description. Deterministic code computes a set of statistics from real, open-source multivariate series; the LLM verbalizes those precomputed facts. Perception, which LLMs do poorly, is handled by computation, while the LLM handles expression. This produces CGTime, our 4B-parameter computation-grounded time-series-language model. CGTime outperforms far larger general-purpose models on multivariate understanding tasks: it attains the best multivariate fact score on our held-out benchmark (0.283 vs. 0.173 for GPT-4o-mini and 0.203 for GPT-5.4-nano), a gap that survives Holm-corrected paired significance tests against every baseline. It also states verifiable numerical facts in generated captions more accurately and covers a broader range of statistical properties.

</details>


### [174] [Disentangling 3D Modeling from Spatial Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05242)
*Haoze Sun,Jiequan Cui,Qingshan Xu,Richang Hong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DiSR框架，通过将3D感知与推理显式解耦，利用现成的感知模型重建结构化3D证据，并用LoRA微调LLM仅基于显式几何证据进行推理，在不进行大规模3D VQA训练的情况下达到具有竞争力的空间推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法依赖大规模训练端到端地隐式学习3D感知与推理，而作者观察到感知模型擅长连续3D几何估计，LLM擅长组合与符号推理，两者互补，因此主张将感知与推理显式分离，作为端到端建模的可扩展替代范式。

Method: 提出Disentangled Spatial Reasoner (DiSR)框架：1) 利用现成的专家感知模型将物理世界重建为结构化3D证据；2) 使用LoRA微调LLM，使其仅基于该显式几何证据进行推理，无需大规模3D VQA训练或复杂工具使用策略。

Result: 在主流空间推理基准上取得具有竞争力的性能，同时带来更好的可解释性、模块化和计算效率。

Conclusion: 显式分离感知与推理是空间智能中一种可扩展且有效的替代范式，优于端到端建模方式。

Abstract: In this work, we explore an alternative paradigm for spatial reasoning by explicitly disentangling 3D perception from reasoning, rather than jointly acquiring implicit 3D perception and reasoning through large-scale training. Our key observation is that modern perception models excel at estimating continuous 3D geometry, whereas large language models (LLMs) are particularly effective at compositional and symbolic reasoning. Motivated by these complementary strengths, we propose the Disentangled Spatial Reasoner (DiSR), a simple yet effective framework that reconstructs the physical world into structured 3D evidence using off-the-shelf expert perception models and fine-tunes an LLM with LoRA to perform reasoning solely over this explicit geometric evidence. Without large-scale 3D VQA training or complex tool-use policies, DiSR achieves competitive performance on popular spatial reasoning benchmarks. Beyond its strong performance, DiSR offers improved interpretability, modularity, and computational efficiency, demonstrating that explicit separation of perception and reasoning is a scalable and effective alternative paradigm to end-to-end modeling for spatial intelligence.

</details>


### [175] [Marginal Matching Does Not License Factorized Sampling: Auditing Conditional Style Leakage in Factorized Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05243)
*Duong Bach,Hai Nguyen Hong,Cuong Do*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出因子化生成模型中将潜在风格变量边缘分布匹配到固定高斯先验并不能保证其与类别信息独立。作者推导出四个必要条件，证明消除这种边缘不匹配是必要的但不充分的，并通过实验证实即使全局MMD接近零，线性探针仍能以74%-100%的准确率恢复类别标签（随机水平10%）。多种缓解策略可将探针准确率降至21%-46%，但类内依赖基本不变。后验条件先验和风格库等方法可改善外部评估的类别条件生成效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 因子化生成模型普遍将风格潜变量的边缘分布正则化为固定高斯先验，并据此声称风格表示与类别信息独立。作者质疑这一解释的正确性，认为仅匹配边缘分布无法约束类别条件分布，潜变量可能仍然高度预测标签。这一误解可能导致对模型因子化能力的虚假信心。

Method: 作者通过数学推导得出因子化采样所需的四个条件，证明边缘分布匹配仅是其中之一。实验上以自研模型和四个代表性潜在基线模型为对象，使用全局MMD、线性探针、聚类准确率和外部类别条件生成评估等指标，测试了六种独立扰动（模型容量、课程学习、先验几何、监督程度）和两种数据集（MNIST、CIFAR-10），并提出了四种缓解策略，以及两种后处理方案（条件先验和风格库）。

Result: 实验发现：1) 所有基线模型全局MMD接近零但线性探针分类准确率高达74%-100%；2) 自研模型聚类准确率达99.15%但外部类别条件生成仅16%成功率；3) 六种扰动下泄漏现象持续存在；4) 四种缓解策略可将探针准确率降至21%-46%但类内依赖不变；5) 后验条件先验在MNIST上达到0.97但CIFAR-10上仅0.41，风格库在CIFAR-10上达0.88。

Conclusion: 仅基于风格潜变量边缘分布的散度度量无法证明其与类别标签的独立性，仅报告边缘统计量不足以验证因子化生成模型中通常声称的性质。研究者需要采用更严格的评估方法（如线性探针或类别条件生成）来验证因子化属性。

Abstract: Factorized generative models commonly regularize a latent style variable z_s by matching its marginal distribution to a fixed Gaussian prior and interpret this as evidence that the style representation is independent of class information. We show that this interpretation is incorrect. Matching only the marginal distribution places no constraint on the class-conditional distributions, allowing the latent style to remain highly predictive of the label despite appearing perfectly Gaussian in aggregate. We derive an exact decomposition showing that this mismatch is one of four conditions required for factorized sampling, and demonstrate that eliminating it is necessary but not sufficient to obtain the intended factorization.
  Empirically, our case-study model and four representative latent baselines achieve near-zero global MMD while still allowing a linear probe to recover class labels with 74%--100% accuracy (10% chance level). Our model reaches 99.15% clustering accuracy, whereas externally evaluated class-conditional generation succeeds only 16% of the time. This leakage remains under six independent perturbations involving model capacity, curriculum, prior geometry, and supervision across two datasets. Four mitigation strategies reduce probe accuracy to 21%--46%, although they leave within-class dependence largely unchanged. A post-hoc conditional prior improves externally evaluated class generation to 0.97 on MNIST without retraining but reaches only 0.41 on CIFAR-10, while an empirical style bank achieves 0.88 on CIFAR-10. These results demonstrate that no divergence computed solely on the marginal distribution of the style latent can certify independence from class labels, and that reporting marginal statistics alone does not verify the property commonly claimed in factorized generative models.

</details>


### [176] [IFlowNets: Extending Generative Samplers to Learn Strategies in Incomplete Information Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05422)
*Conor M. Artman,Nicholas Di,Scott Perkins*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出信息流网络（IFNs），将生成式流网络框架（AFlowNets）从完整信息博弈扩展到不完整信息博弈。作者证明原有约束在不完整信息博弈中无法生成有效密度（对应玩家策略）和有效训练目标，并提出IFlowNets作为严格泛化AFlowNets的解决方案。在三个标准博弈环境中，IFlowNets在性能和速度上优于或媲美OSMCCFR和标准RL方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管已有大量研究将强化学习（RL）与反事实遗憾（CFR）方法结合以权衡计算速度与性能，但在不完整信息博弈中，对生成式采样框架的探索相对匮乏。现有AFlowNets仅适用于完整信息博弈，无法直接迁移至不完整信息场景。

Method: 提出信息流网络（IFNs/IFlowNets），将生成式流网络框架推广至不完整信息博弈。通过证明原有约束在不完整信息博弈中不成立（无法获得对应玩家策略的有效概率密度和有效训练目标），设计新的泛化框架IFlowNets来缓解该问题，并严格泛化AFlowNets。

Result: 在三个标准博弈环境的初步实验中，IFlowNets在性能和速度方面与Outcome Sampling Monte Carlo Counterfactual Regret（OSMCCFR）以及标准RL方法相比，表现相当或更优。

Conclusion: IFlowNets成功克服了AFlowNets在不完整信息博弈中的局限性，为生成式采样框架在博弈论应用中的扩展提供了理论基础与实证支持，在不完整信息博弈中展现出有竞争力的性能与效率。

Abstract: While many algorithms blend reinforcement learning (RL) with counterfactual regret (CFR) methods to leverage tradeoffs in computational speed and performance, there are fewer investigations into generative sampling frameworks in game theoretic applications in incomplete information games. We extend a generative flow network framework, Adversarial Flow Networks (AFlowNets), to incomplete information games, called Information Flow Networks (IFNs). We prove that previously established constraints for generative flow networks in complete information games are inadmissible for obtaining valid densities (corresponding to player strategies) and a valid training objective. We show that our proposed generalization, IFlowNets, alleviates this issue and strictly generalizes AFlowNets. In preliminary results for three standard game environments, IFlowNets perform comparably to or better than Outcome Sampling Monte Carlo Counterfactual Regret (OSMCCFR) and standard RL-based methods in performance and speed.

</details>


### [177] [PRISM: Priority-aware Rubric Internalization via Structured Multimodal Data Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05249)
*Xiaomin He,Dongling Xiao,Jiahao Xie,Ruiqi Lu,Qianle Wang,Zhongbin Guo,Wanxuan Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PRISM框架，通过四阶段数据合成流程（Persona任务对、前缀引导规则集、质量过滤rubric、结构化验证轨迹）训练多模态大模型遵循带优先级的多条规则（rubric comprehension），并引入无需推理时评判模型的PRISM-Eval评估基准。仅用10K合成样本即可将Qwen3-VL-4B的Strict准确率从9.5%提升至30.1%，且性能迁移至其他四种开源MLLM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界的多模态指令通常捆绑多条重要性不等的要求，但现有多模态训练数据大多将指令遵循简化为回答单一自包含问题。作者研究这一差距，强调模型应作为"执行者"而非"生成者"来遵循rubric。

Method: 1) 提出PRISM四阶段数据合成框架：生成persona-task对、前缀引导规则集、质量过滤rubric、结构化验证轨迹；2) 引入PRISM-Eval评估基准，包含Loose和Strict两种指标，采用确定性匹配固定标签，无需推理时的评判模型。

Result: 仅用10K合成样本，PRISM使Qwen3-VL-4B的Strict准确率从9.5%提升到30.1%，同时保持通用基准的平均性能；收益可迁移至四种额外开源MLLM（涵盖密集和MoE架构）。

Conclusion: 结构化rubric监督是迈向多规则、优先级感知的多模态指令遵循的可扩展路径。

Abstract: Real-world multimodal instructions often bundle multiple requirements with unequal importance, yet most multimodal training data still reduce instruction following to answering one self-contained question. We study this gap through \textbf{rubric comprehension}, which casts the model not as a generator measured against rubrics but as an \textbf{executor} that follows them: given an image and a typed, prioritized rubric, the model must verify each rule before producing an overall judgment. To support this setting, we propose \textbf{PRISM}, a four-stage data synthesis framework that produces persona--task pairs, prefix-guided rule sets, quality-filtered rubrics, and structured verification traces. We further introduce \textbf{PRISM-Eval}, whose Loose and Strict metrics use deterministic matching against fixed labels and therefore require no inference-time judge model. With only 10K synthesized samples, PRISM lifts Qwen3-VL-4B from 9.5\% to 30.1\% Strict accuracy on PRISM-Eval while preserving average performance on general benchmarks, and the gains transfer to four additional open-source MLLMs across dense and MoE architectures, suggesting that structured rubric supervision is a scalable path toward multi-rule, priority-aware multimodal instruction following.

</details>


### [178] [Hybrid-Adaptive Thread Tuning to Mitigate Simulation Execution Bottlenecks in High-Performance Reinforcement Learning Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06025)
*Jiming Su,Hantao Hua,Lujia Yin,Yiping Yao,Feng Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出AutoThread，一种面向强化学习(RL)推理中仿真器瓶颈的混合自适应线程调优方法。通过分析发现任务执行时间与调度时间的比率是决定最优线程数的关键因素，据此采用物理信息神经算子(PINO)作为线程数预测器，并结合有限源M/M/1排队模型进行约束和引导预测，同时引入负载感知的在线微调机制补偿预测误差。实验表明相比静态策略平均加速18.4%，吞吐量分别达到XGBoost和Reinforcer的1.7倍和1.8倍，与最先进方法相比执行时间最高减少83.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在仿真-环路决策系统中，强化学习推理常受限于仿真器端的执行开销，工作负载高度动态且对运行时线程配置敏感。现有的多线程策略难以在执行前或执行过程中匹配线程资源，导致资源争用、调度开销增大和吞吐量下降。核心问题是缺乏对动态工作负载下最优线程数的高效准确预测方法。

Method: AutoThread包含三个关键组件：(1) 基于物理信息神经算子(PINO)的线程数预测器，利用任务执行时间与调度时间的比率作为核心特征进行预测；(2) 有限源M/M/1排队模型，用于约束和引导PINO的预测，在动态工作负载下提供快速且准确的估计；(3) 负载感知的在线微调机制，在执行过程中补偿预测误差并细化资源分配。

Result: AutoThread在平均加速比上比静态策略提升18.4%；平均吞吐量分别达到XGBoost方法的1.7倍和Reinforcer方法的1.8倍；与最先进方法相比，执行时间最多减少83.8%。代码和数据集已公开。

Conclusion: 通过将物理启发的排队论模型与神经算子预测相结合，AutoThread成功解决了RL推理中仿真器的动态线程调优难题，证明融合领域知识与深度学习方法可以显著提升仿真-环路决策系统的性能。

Abstract: In simulation-in-the-loop decision-making systems, reinforcement learning (RL) inference is often constrained by simulator-side execution overhead, where workloads are highly dynamic and sensitive to runtime thread configurations. Existing multithreaded strategies struggle to match thread resources before or during execution, causing resource contention, scheduling overhead, and reduced throughput. Through empirical analysis, we identify the ratio of task execution time to scheduling time as the key factor determining the optimal thread count. Building on this insight, we propose AutoThread, a hybrid adaptive thread-tuning method for mitigating simulation bottlenecks in RL inference. AutoThread employs a Physics-Informed Neural Operator (PINO) as a thread-count predictor and incorporates a finite-source M/M/1 queueing model to constrain and guide prediction, enabling fast and accurate estimation under dynamic workloads. It further performs load-aware online fine-tuning to compensate for prediction errors and refine resource allocation. Experiments show that AutoThread improves average speedup by 18.4\% over static strategies, achieves average throughput of 1.7x and 1.8x that of XGBoost and Reinforcer, respectively, and reduces execution time by up to 83.8\% compared with state-of-the-art methods. Our code and dataset are publicly available at https://github.com/suchenjm/AutoThread.

</details>


### [179] [Beyond Full-Model Rollback: AuroSFT for Adapter-State Multi-Task Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05250)
*Yue Han,Ziniu Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出AuroSFT，一种参数高效的过拟合感知多任务监督微调框架。它通过冻结预训练主干网络、仅训练注入的适配器，并将调度器状态压缩为可合并的适配器状态，解决了原msft方法需要存储全模型检查点的高成本问题。AuroSFT在五个主干网络上均取得比msft更高的平均准确率（61.36% vs 59.85%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多任务监督微调将异构数据混合视为单一优化问题，但不同任务在不同时间达到最佳泛化。msft通过任务级展开、排除和回滚来暴露这一不匹配，但其原始形式将调度器状态实现为全模型检查点，导致阶段转换在存储、恢复和部署方面成本高昂。

Method: AuroSFT冻结预训练主干网络，只训练注入的适配器；在任务级峰值回滚适配器检查点，并在剩余活跃混合数据上继续训练。在层级别，每个适配器将AuroRA启发的自适应非线性层应用于低秩权重因子而非样本表示，使更新保持输入线性、秩有界，并可精确合并到冻结投影中。

Result: 在保留主干网络的比较协议下，AuroSFT取得61.36%的平均准确率，而对应的msft参考行仅为59.85%，且在全部五个主干网络上均获得更高准确率。

Conclusion: AuroSFT通过参数高效的方式重新实现了过拟合感知的多任务SFT，在降低存储和部署成本的同时提升了模型性能，代码已开源。

Abstract: Multi-task supervised fine-tuning (SFT) often casts a heterogeneous data mixture as a single optimization problem, even though different tasks may reach their best generalization at different times. msft exposes this mismatch through task-wise roll-out, exclusion, and rollback, but its original formulation materializes the scheduler state as full-model checkpoints, making stage transitions costly to store, restore, and deploy. This paper introduces AuroSFT, a parameter-efficient framework that recasts the carried state of overfitting-aware multi-task SFT as a compact, mergeable adapter state. AuroSFT freezes the pretrained backbone, trains only injected adapters, rolls back adapter checkpoints at task-wise peaks, and continues on the remaining active mixture. At the layer level, each adapter applies an AuroRA-inspired adaptive nonlinear layer to a low-rank weight factor rather than to the sample representation. The resulting update remains linear in the input, rank-bounded, and exactly mergeable into the frozen projection. Under the retained-backbone comparison protocol, AuroSFT achieves 61.36% average accuracy, compared with 59.85% for the corresponding msft reference row, and obtains higher accuracy on all five backbones. Our code is available at the anonymous repository: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/AuroSFT-80D1.

</details>


### [180] [Does Latent Context Help? A Controlled Evaluation of Inverse Reinforcement Learning in Arctic Shipping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06105)
*Vaishnav Vaidheeswaran,Dilith Jayakody,Biruk Ambaw,Jaswanth Kumar,Md Mahbub Alam,Gabriel Spadon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对北极航运中AI辅助导航的逆强化学习（IRL）奖励模型进行了受控评估，利用3,186条AIS船舶轨迹数据比较了线性共享奖励、非线性共享奖励和潜在上下文模型。研究发现非线性奖励比线性基线提升50.9%的似然度，但加入船舶特定潜在上下文反而使性能下降16.5%，表明表面上的船舶行为差异主要由可观测的航线和环境条件解释，而非隐藏的船舶特定因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI辅助导航在北极航运中需要可解释且对变化环境稳健的奖励模型。虽然元IRL方法引入潜在上下文变量以捕捉行为异质性，但不清楚这些潜在表示是否真正恢复了隐藏偏好，还是仅仅重新编码了已在观测状态中可用的信息。这关系到AI在安全关键领域的可信部署。

Method: 对来自202艘船舶、覆盖9个北极航运季节的3,186条AIS轨迹进行了受控评估。比较了三种模型：线性共享奖励、非线性共享奖励、以及基于相同非线性架构的潜在上下文模型。通过行为分析、上下文探测和预注册的特征隐藏消融实验来检验行为变异来源。

Result: 非线性奖励在留出似然度上比线性基线提升50.9%；添加船舶特定潜在上下文反而使性能降低16.5%。行为分析和特征隐藏消融表明，表面上的船舶级变异主要由可观测的路线和环境条件解释。此外，预测精度、路线保真度和奖励迁移产生了不同的模型排名，证明单一指标不足以评估学习到的奖励。

Conclusion: 在添加每船舶潜在上下文之前，应首先测试可观测的路线、环境和船舶特征是否已能解释行为变异。这一发现支持在安全关键领域更可信地部署AI系统，并对北极航运AI导航的奖励模型设计提出了重要指导。

Abstract: Artificial Intelligence (AI)-assisted navigation can help Arctic shipping adapt to rapidly changing sea-ice conditions, but reliable deployment requires reward models that are interpretable and robust to changing environments. Inverse reinforcement learning (IRL) provides a framework for recovering such rewards from vessel trajectories, while recent meta-IRL methods introduce latent context variables to capture behavioral heterogeneity. However, it remains unclear whether these latent representations recover genuinely hidden preferences or simply re-encode information already available in the observed state. We conduct a controlled evaluation on 3,186 AIS-derived voyages from 202 vessels across nine Arctic shipping seasons, comparing a linear shared reward, a nonlinear shared reward, and a latent-context model built on the same nonlinear architecture. The nonlinear reward improves held-out likelihood by 50.9% over the linear baseline, whereas adding vessel-specific latent context reduces performance by 16.5%. Behavioral analysis, context probes, and a pre-registered feature-hiding ablation show that apparent vessel-level variation is largely explained by observable route and environmental conditions rather than hidden vessel-specific factors. Moreover, predictive accuracy, route fidelity, and reward transfer yield different model rankings, demonstrating that no single metric is sufficient to evaluate learned rewards. These findings motivate testing whether the observed route, environmental, and vessel features already explain behavioral variation before adding per-vessel latent context. This supports more trustworthy AI deployment in safety-critical domains.

</details>


### [181] [Beyond Rotations: AuroOFT for Expressive Quantized Orthogonal Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05253)
*Yue Han,Dianlin Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出AuroOFT，一种参数高效微调方法，在量化正交微调（qoft）基础上附加零起始门控低秩非线性残差分支，使更新不再局限于线性正交变换，从而提升低比特语言模型的适配能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: qoft虽能实现参数高效的量化适配，但其任务特定更新被限制在线性正交变换范围内，无法进行依赖输入的非线性修正，限制了模型适配性能。

Method: AuroOFT保留qoft作为稳定的量化兼容分支，在每个适配的线性层上附加一个零起始门控低秩非线性残差。激活经过RMS归一化映射到紧凑潜在空间，使用自适应非线性基和受限或token依赖门控，零初始化的上投影使AuroOFT在初始化时与qoft功能等价。

Result: 在匹配的数据、优化、解码和解析协议下，AuroOFT相比qoft在1.5B/3B Qwen2.5设置上Macro-6提升1.30-2.70%，超过QLoRA 6.52-10.62%，且相对QLoRA节省32.3-44.7%可训练参数。

Conclusion: AuroOFT通过引入非线性残差分支在保持量化稳定性的同时突破线性变换限制，实现性能提升和参数效率的双重优势。

Abstract: Quantized orthogonal fine-tuning (qoft) enables parameter-efficient adaptation of low-bit language models by learning structured activation rotations before frozen quantized weights. However, its task-specific updates remain constrained to linear orthogonal transformations, limiting input-dependent nonlinear corrections. We introduce AuroOFT, which keeps qoft as a stable quantization-compatible branch while attaching a zero-start gated low-rank nonlinear residual to each adapted linear layer. AuroOFT maps activations into an RMS-normalized compact latent space and uses adaptive nonlinear bases with bounded or token-dependent gating. The zero-initialized up projection makes AuroOFT functionally identical to qoft at initialization, while orthogonality remains a branch-level stability property rather than a property of the combined nonlinear layer. Under matched data, optimization, decoding, and parser protocols, AuroOFT improves Macro-6 over matched qoft by 1.30-2.70% on the 1.5B/3B Qwen2.5 settings, exceeds QLoRA by 6.52-10.62%, and saves 32.3-44.7% trainable parameters relative to QLoRA in representative scales. The small exam-style multiple-choice math set is treated only as a protocol-sensitivity diagnostic. Our code is available at the anonymous repository: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/AuroOFT-F3FD.

</details>


### [182] [An Emerging Retail Portfolio Management Application: Personalized, Tax-Aware Reinforcement Learning with Natural Language Goals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05255)
*Ramin Pishehvar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个面向散户投资者的个性化税收感知投资组合管理应用，采用FastAPI后端加Web仪表盘，通过三阶段强化学习系统（自监督跨资产编码器、MoE分配策略、LoRA适配器）将自然语言投资目标转化为实时、券商集成的投资组合推荐。系统已在Alpaca纸上交易API上完成端到端集成测试，但尚未面向真实用户部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 散户投资者缺乏机构客户享有的个性化、税收感知的投资组合管理服务：现有机器人顾问仅使用静态规则配置，而机构级系统有最低账户门槛和技术栈要求，个人投资者无法使用。

Method: 三阶段强化学习系统：(1) 自监督跨资产编码器，(2) 带学习意图路由器的Mixture-of-Experts（MoE）分配策略，(3) 轻量LoRA适配器，从用户披露的券商行为中个性化推荐而无需重训练共享模型。系统还包括FastAPI后端、Web仪表盘、六种投资任务路由、多用户认证、预览-确认流程、每日邮件摘要和可审计操作完整性链。

Result: 系统功能完整，已针对Alpaca纸交易API完成端到端集成测试（含多用户认证等）。进行了14天滚动前向回测（含自助置信区间）作为部署前初步验证。报告了三条工程经验：静默不活跃的集成路径、挂起的第三方API调用、端到端实证验证优于信任检查点元数据。
